HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000566/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_380e3569ac3573290735","text":"2. **Watching as prepared accounting.** **Evidence/cue:** `ر ص د B002` is preparing or setting something aside for a debt or recompense, and `ر ب ب B001` supplies governing authority. The forward context repeatedly measures what is allotted, withheld, fed, or inherited. **Mechanism:** the watchpost stores an answer corresponding to each transfer, functioning as prior preparation rather than delayed reaction. The packet supplies allocation language; the debt-and-recompense direction is inferred. **Reading change:** *bil-mirsad* means active readiness to answer conduct, not passive awareness of it.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000566/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3a383d78c4cd7ce7a773","text":"The later measured provision in 89:16 made `ر ص د B002` important: the watcher is also the one who has prepared and measured outcomes.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000566/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3d39819a6645568a2ae0","text":"2. **Prepared recompense rather than sudden reaction.** Lexical evidence: `ر ص د B002` preparing or setting aside for recompense; `ق د ر B005` later arranging by estimate; `ب ل و B002` later testing and disclosure. Structural cue: this ayah stands between past nations and the human test. Assigned role: the lookout is also a prepared accounting station. Abductive move: I infer that judgment has been set aside in advance because the next ayat show testing under measured provision. Reading change: the ayah reads as an administrative principle, not only a threat.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B001","root_000566/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3ecfe4da8b036b397f5d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"89:14:2\",\"root_000532\",[\"B001\"]],[\"89:14:3\",\"root_000566\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Gözetleme yeri, davranışın karşılığını sonradan düşünen bir makam değil, borç ve karşılığı önceden hazırlayan bir hesap noktasıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000566/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_618590b6ec4a40da771f","text":"1. **A watchpost placed on routes of movement and transfer.** **Evidence/cue:** `ر ص د B001/B003` supplies watching from a lookout or path. The window begins with a carved valley-route in 89:9 and moves forward through provision, orphan-care, feeding, and inherited property in 89:15-19. **Mechanism:** the lookout intercepts not only imperial movement through land but the ordinary movement of goods between persons. The packet supplies both route-types; I infer that the fixed construction spans them. **Reading change:** divine watch is stationed at the passages where power and wealth actually circulate, not at a remote observational point.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000566/B001","root_000566/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_89cf9294167de33f36d4","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"89:14:3\",\"root_000566\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Gözetleme, yalnız uzak bir bakış değil, iktidarın ve malın geçtiği yollar üzerinde kurulmuş etkin bir denetim noktasıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000566/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c3a9240fa0aa55d53b7b","text":"89:15-19 moved the watchpost from imperial roads onto routes of bounty, food, and inheritance. The inheritance ayah was most surprising because `ر ص د B002` already includes setting something aside for debt or recompense, turning observation into prepared accounting of transfers.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B001","root_000566/B001","root_000566/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_fe698a5579d55090967a","text":"1. **Lordship stationed on the path.** Lexical evidence: `ر ب ب B001` lordship, ownership, authority; `ر ص د B001` watching, waiting, guarding; `ر ص د B003` lookout place, watching post, or path. Structural cue: after historical punishments, the ayah states a constant surveillance position. Assigned role: `المرصاد` is the spatial station where transgression cannot pass unobserved.
